utils.py\nimport os\nimport importlib.util\nimport csv\nimport math\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Import global configuration\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, '/hada')\nfrom config import set_global_seed, SEED, MODEL_NAME\n\n# Set global seed at module load time\nset_global_seed()\n\n\ndef load_opt_vals():\n    \"\"\"\u52a0\u8f7d dataset.csv \u6587\u4ef6\uff0c\u8fd4\u56de\u4e00\u4e2a\u5b57\u5178 {(function, instance, dimension): opt_val}\"\"\"\n    dataset_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'dataset.csv')\n    opt_vals = {}\n    if os.path.exists(dataset_path):\n        with open(dataset_path, 'r') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for row in reader:\n                key = (int(row['function']), int(row['instance']), int(row['dimension']))\n                opt_vals[key] = float(row['opt_val'])\n    return opt_vals\n\n\ndef compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val):\n    \"\"\"\u8ba1\u7b97 score = (initial_best - best) / (initial_best - opt)\u3002\n    \n    \u5176\u4e2d\uff1a\n    - initial_best = initial_gbest_f\uff08PSO\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u540e\u79cd\u7fa4\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - best = gbest_f\uff08PSO\u8fd0\u884c\u7ed3\u675f\u540e\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - opt = opt_val\uff08\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    \n    \u8fd4\u56de\u622a\u65ad\u5230 [0, 1] \u7684 score\u3002\n    \"\"\"    \n    if abs(initial_gbest_f - opt_val) < 1e-12:\n        return 1.0 if gbest_f <= opt_val + 1e-12 else 0.0\n    \n    score = (initial_gbest_f - gbest_f) / (initial_gbest_f - opt_val)\n    return max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n\ndef run_de_and_score(inputs, log_fn=print, mode='test', controller=None, seed=None):\n    \"\"\"\u8fd0\u884c DQN \u63a7\u5236\u7684 DE \u5e76\u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u3002\n    \n    Args:\n        inputs: dict from domains.bbob_unconstrained.utils.format_input_dict\n        Expected keys: 'problem_id','dimension','instance'\n        log_fn: logging function\n        mode: 'train' for training DQN (updates model), 'test' for evaluation only\n        controller: optional external controller (shared across problems)\n        seed: random seed for DE initialization\n    \n    Returns:\n        prediction (string) in format \"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\"\n    \"\"\"\n    # lazy import cocoex and DE module from metabbo\n    import cocoex\n\n    this_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', '..', 'metabbo')\n    # \u5c06 metabbo \u76ee\u5f55\u6dfb\u52a0\u5230 sys.path\uff0c\u4ee5\u4fbf\u6a21\u5757\u5185\u7684\u76f8\u5bf9\u5bfc\u5165\u53ef\u4ee5\u6b63\u5e38\u5de5\u4f5c\n    if this_dir not in __import__('sys').path:\n        __import__('sys').path.insert(0, this_dir)\n    # load local ec_algorithm module\n    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ec_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'ec_algorithm.py'))\n    ec_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    spec.loader.exec_module(ec_mod)\n    \n    # Create or use provided controller\n    if controller is None:\n        # load meta learning implementations\n        spec2 = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('meta_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'meta_learning.py'))\n        meta_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec2)\n        spec2.loader.exec_module(meta_mod)\n        model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n        controller = meta_mod.DiscreteDQNController(model_path=model_path, training=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n    \n    # prepare suite filter (dimension + instance)\n    dim = int(inputs.get('dimension', 10))\n    inst = int(inputs.get('instance', 1))\n    max_evals = max(1000, dim * 500)\n\n    # \u4ece problem_id \u5b57\u7b26\u4e32\u4e2d\u63d0\u53d6 function \u7f16\u53f7\uff08\u4f8b\u5982 f1_i1 -> 1\uff09\n    problem_id_str = inputs.get('problem_id', 'f1_i1')\n    problem_id = int(str(problem_id_str).replace('f', '').split('_')[0])\n\n    suite = cocoex.Suite('bbob', '', f'dimensions: {dim} instance_indices:{inst} function_indices: {problem_id}')\n    # pick the matching problem from the suite\n    problem = next(iter(suite))\n\n    # run DE algorithm using DEOptimizer to get stats\n    try:\n        de_opt = ec_mod.DEOptimizer(problem.dimension, getattr(problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(problem, 'upper_bounds', None), max_evals, params={'F': 0.5, 'CR': 0.5}, controller=controller, recorder=None, swarm_size=40)\n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4f1a\u66f4\u65b0 DQN \u6a21\u578b\uff0c\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6a21\u5f0f\u53ea\u4f7f\u7528\u6a21\u578b\n        gbest, stats = de_opt.optimize(problem, train=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n        gbest_f = stats.get('gbest_f', None)\n        initial_gbest_f = stats.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f)\n        \n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n            import torch\n            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Model saved to {model_path}\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        return f'error: DE run failed: {e}'\n\n    # \u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u5206\u6570\n    # \u52a0\u8f7d\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\n    opt_vals = load_opt_vals()\n    opt_val_key = (problem_id, inst, dim)\n    opt_val = opt_vals.get(opt_val_key, None)\n    \n    # \u4f7f\u7528 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u65b9\u5f0f\n    score = compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val)\n    \n    mode_str = \"TRAIN\" if mode == 'train' else \"TEST\"\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} Score: {score}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} gbest_f: {gbest_f}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} initial_gbest_f: {initial_gbest_f}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} opt_val: {opt_val}\")\n    \n    # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u6536\u96c6 loss \u548c return\n    if mode == 'train':\n        training_loss = controller.get_total_loss() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_loss') else None\n        sweep_return = controller.get_total_reward() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_reward') else None\n        if training_loss is not None:\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN loss: {training_loss:.6f}\")\n        if sweep_return is not None:\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN return: {sweep_return:.4f}\")\n        return {\n            'score': score,\n            'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'opt_val': opt_val,\n            'training_loss': training_loss,\n            'sweep_return': sweep_return,\n            'controller': controller\n        }\n    \n    # \u8fd4\u56de\u683c\u5f0f\uff1a\"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\" \u4ee5\u4fbf report.py \u89e3\u6790\n    prediction = f\"{score}|{gbest_f}|{initial_gbest_f}|{opt_val}\"\n\n    # \u4e5f\u8fd4\u56de dict \u683c\u5f0f\u4f9b\u6d4b\u8bd5\u811a\u672c\u4f7f\u7528\n    return {\n        'score': score,\n        'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n        'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n        'opt_val': opt_val,\n        'training_loss': None,\n        'sweep_return': controller.get_total_reward() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_reward') else None,\n        'controller': controller\n    }\"\n    }\n    </json>",

         opt_vals[key] = float(row['opt_val'])\n    return opt_vals\n\n\ndef compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val):\n    \"\"\"\u8ba1\u7b97 score = (initial_best - best) / (initial_best - opt)\u3002\n    \n    \u5176\u4e2d\uff1a\n    - initial_best = initial_gbest_f\uff08PSO\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u540e\u79cd\u7fa4\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - best = gbest_f\uff08PSO\u8fd0\u884c\u7ed3\u675f\u540e\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    - opt = opt_val\uff08\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    \n    \u8fd4\u56de\u622a\u65ad\u5230 [0, 1] \u7684 score\u3002\n    \"\"\"    \n    if abs(initial_gbest_f - opt_val) < 1e-12:\n        return 1.0 if gbest_f <= opt_val + 1e-12 else 0.0\n    \n    score = (initial_gbest_f - gbest_f) / (initial_gbest_f - opt_val)\n    return max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n\n\ndef run_de_and_score(inputs, log_fn=print, mode='test', controller=None, seed=None):\n    \"\"\"\u8fd0\u884c DQN \u63a7\u5236\u7684 DE     spec2 = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('meta_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'meta_learning.py'))\n        meta_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec2)\n int(str(problem_id_str).replace('f', '').split('_')[0])\n\n    suite = cocoex.Suite('bbob', '', f'dimensions: {dim} instance_indices:{inst} function_indices: {problem_id}')\n    # pick the matching hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n            import torch\n            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n f{problem_id} opt_val: {opt_val}\")\n    \n    # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u6536\u96c6 loss \u548c return\n    if mode == 'train':\n        training_loss = controller.get_total_loss() if   'score': score,\n            'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'opt_val': opt_val,\n            'training_loss': training_loss,\n \u89e3\u6790\n    prediction = f\"{score}|{gbest_f}|{initial_gbest_f}|{opt_val}\"\n\n    # \u4e5f\u8fd4\u56de dict \u683c\u5f0f\u4f9b\u6d4b\u8bd5\u811a\u672c\u4f7f\u7528\n    return {\n        'score': score,\n        'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n        'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n        'opt_val': opt_val,\n        'training_loss': None,\n        'sweep_return': It can load/save a model\n     6\tand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport os\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\ttry:\n    12\t    import torch\n    13\t    import torch.nn as nn\n    14\t    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    15\texcept Exception:\n    16\t    torch = None\n    17\t\n    18\tfrom param_controller import ParamController\n    19\t\n    20\timport sys\n    21\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    22\tfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n    23\t\n    24\tif torch is not None:\n    25\t    class _Net(nn.Module):\n    26\t        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n    27\t            super().__init__()\n    28\t            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n    29\t            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    30\t            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    31\t            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n    32\t            \n    33\t            # \u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n    34\t            if seed is not None:\n    35\t                self._init_weights(seed)\n    36\t\n    37\t        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n    38\t            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n    39\t            g = torch.Generator()\n    40\t            g.manual_seed(seed)\n    41\t            \n    42\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\te: int = 64,\n   136\t                lr: float = 1e-4, gamma: float = 0.99, target_update_freq: int = 50,\n   137\t                max_grad_norm: float = 1.0, epsilon_start: float = 1.0,\n   138\t                epsilon_end: float = 0.05, epsilon_decay: float = 0.999,\n   139\t                seed: int = None):\n   140\t        if torch is None:\n   141\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DiscreteDQNController')\n   142\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n   143\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n   144\t        self.bins = int(bins)\n   145\t        self.seed = seed if seed is not None else 42\n   146\t        \n   147\t        # \u8bbe\u7f6ePyTorch\u79cd\u5b50\u786e\u4fdd\u7f51\u7edc\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u53ef\u590d\u73b0\n   148\t        torch.manual_seed(self.seed)\n   149\t        if torch.cuda.is_available():\n   150\t            torch.cuda.manual_seed(self.seed)\n   151\t            torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(self.seed)\n   152\t        \n   153\t        # \u786e\u4fddPyTorch\u64cd\u4f5c\u5b8c\u5168\u786e\u5b9a\u6027\n   154\t        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n   155\t        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\n   156\t        torch.backends.cudnn.enabled = False  # \u7981\u7528cudnn\u4ee5\u5b8c\u5168\u6d88\u9664\u968f\u673a\u6027\n   157\t        \n   158\t        self.rng = np.random.RandomState(self.seed)\n   159\t\n   160\t        # DE parameter discretization\n   161\t        self.F_vals = np.linspace(0.1, 1.0, self.bins)\n   162\t        self.CR_vals = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, self.bins)\n   163\t\n   164\t        self.actions = []\n   165\t        for fi in self.F_vals:\n   166\t            for cri in self.CR_vals:\n   167\t                self.actions.append({'F': float(fi), 'CR': float(cri)})\n   168\t        self.n_actions = len(self.actions)\n   169\t        \n   170\t        # \u4f7f\u7528\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\n   171\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n   172\t\n   173\t        # Training setup\n   174\t        self.training = training\n   175\t        self.update_freq = update_freq\n   176\t        self.batch_size = batch_size\n   177\t        self.gamma = gamma\n   178\t        self.target_update_freq = target_update_freq\n   179\t        self.step_count = 0\n   180\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   181\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   182\t        self.loss_history = []\n   183\t        self.reward_history = []\n   184\t        self.return_record_interval = 5\n   185\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   186\t\n   187\t        self.max_grad_norm = max_grad_norm\n   188\t        self.epsilon = epsilon_start\n   189\t        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n   190\t        self.epsilon_decay = epsilon_decay\n   191\t        if training:\n   192\t            # It can load/save a model\n     6\tand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport os\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\ttry:\n    12\t    import torch\n    13\t    import torch.nn as nn\n    14\t    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    15\texcept Exception:\n    16\t    torch = None\n    17\t\n    18\tfrom param_controller import ParamController\n    19\t\n    20\timport sys\n    21\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    22\tfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n    23\t\n    24\tif torch is not None:\n    25\t    class _Net(nn.Module):\n    26\t        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n    27\t            super().__init__()\n    28\t            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n    29\t            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    30\t            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    31\t            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n    32\t            \n    33\t            # \u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n    34\t            if seed is not None:\n    35\t                self._init_weights(seed)\n    36\t\n    37\t        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n    38\t            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n    39\t            g = torch.Generator()\n    40\t            g.manual_seed(seed)\n    41\t            \n    42\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\te: int = 64,\n   136\t                lr: float = 1e-4, gamma: float = 0.99, target_update_freq: int = 50,\n   137\t                max_grad_norm: float = 1.0, epsilon_start: float = 1.0,\n   138\t                epsilon_end: float = 0.05, epsilon_decay: float = 0.999,\n   139\t                seed: int = None):\n   140\t        if torch is None:\n   141\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DiscreteDQNController')\n   142\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n   143\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n   144\t        self.bins = int(bins)\n   145\t        self.seed = seed if seed is not None else 42\n   146\t        \n   147\t        # \u7981\u7528cudnn\u4ee5\u5b8c\u5168\u6d88\u9664\u968f\u673a\u6027\n   157\t        \n   158\t        self.rng = np.random.RandomState(self.seed)\n   159\t\n   160\t        # DE parameter discretization\n   161\t        self.F_vals = np.linspace(0.1, 1.0, self.bins)\n   162\t        self.CR_vals = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, self.bins)\n   163\t\n   164\t        self.actions = []\n   165\t        for fi in self.F_vals:\n   166\t            for cri in self.CR_vals:\n   167\t                self.actions.append({'F': float(fi), 'CR': float(cri)})\n   168\t        self.n_actions = len(self.actions)\n   169\t        \n   170\t        # Tuple\nimport numpy as np\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n# Import SEED from config if available\n_config_seed = None\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom x.ndim == 1:\n            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n        result = problem.func(x)\n        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    else:\n        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n        return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during             swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # ---- SHADE (Success-History based Adaptive DE) ----\n        # Memory of successful F and CR values\n        H = 6\n        MF = np.full(H, 0.5)\n        MCR = np.full(H, 0.5)\n        k_mem = 0\n\n        # External archive of replaced individuals\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n        archive_cap = max(1, pop_size)\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n\n            # Sort indices by fitness to support pbest selection\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n            pbest_num = max(1, int(0.2 * pop_size))\n\n            # Per-individual parameters and success records\n            F_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            CR_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            S_F = []\n            S_CR = []\n            S_delta = []\n\n            # Generate trial vectors using current-to-pbest/1 with archive\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Sample F and CR from SHADE memory distributions\n                mi = int(rng.randint(H))\n                F = float(np.clip(rng.standard_cauchy() * 0.1 + MF[mi], 0.0, 1.0))\n                if not np.isfinite(F) or F <= 0.0:\n                    F = base_F\n                F_vals[i] = float(np.clip(F, 0.05, 1.0))\n\n                CR = float(np.clip(rng.normal(MCR[mi], 0.1), 0.0, 1.0))\n                if not np.isfinite(CR):\n                    CR = base_CR\n                CR_vals[i] = float(np.clip(CR, 0.0, 1.0))\n\n                # Choose pbest (best 20% individuals)\n                pi = sorted_idx[rng.randint(pbest_num)]\n\n                # Choose r1 from population excluding current i\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n                if len(candidates) < 1:\n                    continue\n                r1 = int(rng.choice(candidates))\n\n                # Choose r2 from archive (with probability) or from population\n                use_archive = len(archive) > 0 and rng.rand() < 0.5\n                if use_archive:\n                    idx2 = int(rng.randint(len(archive)))\n                    x_r2 = archive[idx2]\n                else:\n                    # ensure r2 distinct from i and r1 when selecting from population\n                    candidates2 = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != r1]\n                    if len(candidates2) < 1:\n                        continue\n                    r2 = int(rng.choice(candidates2))\n                    x_r2 = pop[r2]\n\n                xi = pop[i]\n                x_pbest = pop[pi]\n                x_r1 = pop[r1]\n\n                # current-to-pbest/1 with optional archive\n                v = xi + F_vals[i] * (x_pbest - xi) + F_vals[i] * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover\n                u = np.copy(xi)\n                j_rand = rng.randint(self.dim)\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.rand() < CR_vals[i] or j == j_rand:\n                        u[j] = v[j]\n\n                # Boundary handling: random reinitialization (better than clipping for multimodal problems)\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if u[j] < lb[j] or u[j] > ub[j]:\n                        u[j] = lb[j] + rng.rand() * (ub[j] - lb[j])\n\n                new_pop[i] = u\n\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                old_f = float(fitness[i])\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n                \n                # Greedy selection with archive update\n                if trial_f < old_f:\n                    # Add replaced individual to archive\n                    if len(archive) < archive_cap:\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i]])\n                    else:\n                        ar = rng.randint(archive_cap)\n                        archive[ar] = pop[i]\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n\n                    # Record successful parameters for memory update\n                    S_F.append(F_vals[i])\n                    S_CR.append(CR_vals[i])\n                    S_delta.append(max(1e-30, abs(old_f - trial_f)))\n\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update SHADE success-history memories\n            if len(S_F) > 0:\n                s_f = np.asarray(S_F)\n                s_cr = np.asarray(S_CR)\n                w = np.asarray(S_delta, dtype=float)\n                w = w / np.sum(w)\n\n                # Weighted Lehmer mean for F\n                num = float(np.sum(w * s_f * s_f))\n                den = float(np.sum(w * s_f))\n                if den > 1e-12:\n                    MF[k_mem] = num / den\n                else:\n                    MF[k_mem] = 0.5\n\n                # Weighted arithmetic mean for CR\n                MCR[k_mem] = float(np.sum(w * s_cr))\n\n                k_mem = (k_mem + 1) % H\n\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n The algorithm accepts an optional `controller`\nobject implementing the ParamController interface to allow dynamic parameter\nadjustment (F, CR).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\nimport numpy as np\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n# Import SEED from config if available\n_config_seed = None\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n\n\ndef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    \n    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n        if x.ndim == 1:\n            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n        result = problem.func(x)\n        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    else:\n        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n        return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n                 swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # ---- SHADE (Success-History based Adaptive DE) ----\n        # Memory of successful F and CR values\n        H = 6\n        MF = np.full(H, 0.5)\n        MCR = np.full(H, 0.5)\n        k_mem = 0\n\n        # External archive of replaced individuals\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n        archive_cap = max(1, pop_size)\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n\n            # Sort indices by fitness to support pbest selection\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n            pbest_num = max(1, int(0.2 * pop_size))\n\n            # Per-individual parameters and success records\n            F_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            CR_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            S_F = []\n            S_CR = []\n            S_delta = []\n\n            # Generate trial vectors using current-to-pbest/1 with archive\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Sample F and CR from SHADE memory distributions\n                mi = int(rng.randint(H))\n                F = float(np.clip(rng.standard_cauchy() * 0.1 + MF[mi], 0.0, 1.0))\n                if not np.isfinite(F) or F <= 0.0:\n                    F = base_F\n                F_vals[i] = float(np.clip(F, 0.05, 1.0))\n\n                CR = float(np.clip(rng.normal(MCR[mi], 0.1), 0.0, 1.0))\n                if not np.isfinite(CR):\n                    CR = base_CR\n                CR_vals[i] = float(np.clip(CR, 0.0, 1.0))\n\n                # Choose pbest (best 20% individuals)\n                pi = sorted_idx[rng.randint(pbest_num)]\n\n                # Choose r1 from population excluding current i\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n                if len(candidates) < 1:\n                    continue\n                r1 = int(rng.choice(candidates))\n\n                # Choose r2 from archive (with probability) or from population\n                use_archive = len(archive) > 0 and rng.rand() < 0.5\n                if use_archive:\n                    idx2 = int(rng.randint(len(archive)))\n                    x_r2 = archive[idx2]\n                else:\n                    # ensure r2 distinct from i and r1 when selecting from population\n                    candidates2 = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != r1]\n                    if len(candidates2) < 1:\n                        continue\n                    r2 = int(rng.choice(candidates2))\n                    x_r2 = pop[r2]\n\n                xi = pop[i]\n                x_pbest = pop[pi]\n                x_r1 = pop[r1]\n\n                # current-to-pbest/1 with optional archive\n                v = xi + F_vals[i] * (x_pbest - xi) + F_vals[i] * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover\n                u = np.copy(xi)\n                j_rand = rng.randint(self.dim)\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.rand() < CR_vals[i] or j == j_rand:\n                        u[j] = v[j]\n\n                # Boundary handling: random reinitialization (better than clipping for multimodal problems)\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if u[j] < lb[j] or u[j] > ub[j]:\n                        u[j] = lb[j] + rng.rand() * (ub[j] - lb[j])\n\n                new_pop[i] = u\n\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                old_f = float(fitness[i])\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n                \n                # Greedy selection with archive update\n                if trial_f < old_f:\n                    # Add replaced individual to archive\n                    if len(archive) < archive_cap:\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i]])\n                    else:\n                        ar = rng.randint(archive_cap)\n                        archive[ar] = pop[i]\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n\n                    # Record successful parameters for memory update\n                    S_F.append(F_vals[i])\n                    S_CR.append(CR_vals[i])\n                    S_delta.append(max(1e-30, abs(old_f - trial_f)))\n\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update SHADE success-history memories\n            if len(S_F) > 0:\n                s_f = np.asarray(S_F)\n                s_cr = np.asarray(S_CR)\n                w = np.asarray(S_delta, dtype=float)\n                w = w / np.sum(w)\n\n                # Weighted Lehmer mean for F\n                num = float(np.sum(w * s_f * s_f))\n                den = float(np.sum(w * s_f))\n                if den > 1e-12:\n                    MF[k_mem] = num / den\n                else:\n                    MF[k_mem] = 0.5\n\n                # Weighted arithmetic mean for CR\n                MCR[k_mem] = float(np.sum(w * s_cr))\n\n                k_mem = (k_mem + 1) % H\n\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n        }\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>"

Memory of successful F and CR values\n        H = 6\n        MF = np.full(H, 0.5)\n        MCR = np.full(H, 0.5)\n        k_mem = 0\n\n        # External archive of replaced individuals\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n        archive_cap = max(1, pop_size)\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            pbest_ratio = float(params.get('pbest_ratio', 0.2))\n            archive_ratio = float(params.get('archive_ratio', 1.0))\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n\n            # Sort indices by fitness to support pbest selection\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n            pbest_num = max(1, int(pbest_ratio * pop_size))\n\n            # Per-individual parameters and success records\n            F_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            CR_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n            S_F = []\n            S_CR = []\n            S_delta = []\n\n            # Generate trial vectors using current-to-pbest/1 with archive\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Sample F and CR from SHADE memory distributions\n                mi = int(rng.randint(H))\n                F = float(np.clip(rng.standard_cauchy() * 0.1 + MF[mi], 0.0, 1.0))\n                if not np.isfinite(F) or F <= 0.0:\n                    F = base_F\n                F_vals[i] = float(np.clip(F, 0.05, 1.0))\n\n                CR = float(np.clip(rng.normal(MCR[mi], 0.1), 0.0, 1.0))\n                if not np.isfinite(CR):\n                    CR = base_CR\n                CR_vals[i] = float(np.clip(CR, 0.0, 1.0))\n\n                # Choose pbest (best 20% individuals)\n                pi = sorted_idx[rng.randint(pbest_num)]\n\n                # Choose r1 from population excluding current i\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n                if len(candidates) < 1:\n                    continue\n                r1 = int(rng.choice(candidates))\n\n                # Choose r2 from archive (with probability) or from population\n                use_archive = len(archive) > 0 and rng.rand() < 0.5\n                if use_archive:\n                    idx2 = int(rng.randint(len(archive)))\n                    x_r2 = archive[idx2]\n                else:\n                    # ensure r2 distinct from i and r1 when selecting from population\n                    candidates2 = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != r1]\n                    if len(candidates2) < 1:\n                        continue\n                    r2 = int(rng.choice(candidates2))\n                    x_r2 = pop[r2]\n\n                xi = pop[i]\n                x_pbest = pop[pi]\n                x_r1 = pop[r1]\n\n                # current-to-pbest/1 with optional archive\n                v = xi + F_vals[i] * (x_pbest - xi) + F_vals[i] * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover\n                u = np.copy(xi)\n                j_rand = rng.randint(self.dim)\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.rand() < CR_vals[i] or j == j_rand:\n                        u[j] = v[j]\n\n                # Boundary handling: random reinitialization (better than clipping for multimodal problems)\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if u[j] < lb[j] or u[j] > ub[j]:\n                        u[j] = lb[j] + rng.rand() * (ub[j] - lb[j])\n\n                new_pop[i] = u\n\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                old_f = float(fitness[i])\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n                \n                # Greedy selection with archive update\n                if trial_f < old_f:\n                    # Add replaced individual to archive\n                    if len(archive) < archive_cap:\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i]])\n                    else:\n                        ar = rng.randint(archive_cap)\n                        archive[ar] = pop[i]\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n\n                    # Record successful parameters for memory update\n                    S_F.append(F_vals[i])\n                    S_CR.append(CR_vals[i])\n                    S_delta.append(max(1e-30, abs(old_f - trial_f)))\n\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update SHADE success-history memories\n            if len(S_F) > 0:\n                s_f = np.asarray(S_F)\n                s_cr = np.asarray(S_CR)\n                w = np.asarray(S_delta, dtype=float)\n                w = w / np.sum(w)\n\n                # Weighted Lehmer mean for F\n                num = float(np.sum(w * s_f * s_f))\n                den = float(np.sum(w * s_f))\n                if den > 1e-12:\n                    MF[k_mem] = num / den\n                else:\n                    MF[k_mem] = 0.5\n\n                # Weighted arithmetic mean for CR\n                MCR[k_mem] = float(np.sum(w * s_cr))\n\n                k_mem = (k_mem + 1) % H\n\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n        }\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>",

# cocoex style: problem(x)\n    33\t        return float(problem(x))\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    37\t    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    67\t\n    68\t    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n    69\t        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n    70\t 80\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n    81\t        if self.controller is not None:\n    82\t            self.controller.reset({'dim':     ub = self.upper_bounds\n    87\t        \n    88\t        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n    89\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n    90\t\n int(np.argmin(fitness))\n   106\t        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n   107\t        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n   108\t        \n   109\t        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n   110\t        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n   111\t        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n   112\t\n   113\t        # ---- SHADE (Success-History based Adaptive DE) ----\n   114\t        # Memory of successful F and CR values\n   115\t        H = 6\n   116\t        MF = np.full(H, 0.5)\n   117\t        MCR = np.full(H, 0.5)\n   118\t        k_mem = 0\n   119\t\n   120\t        # External archive of replaced individuals\n   121\t        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n   122\t        archive_cap = max(1, pop_size)\n   123\t\n   124\t        # Main loop\n   125\t if self.controller is not None:\n   132\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   133\t                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 144\t\n   145\t            # Sort indices by fitness to support pbest selection\n   146\t            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n   147\t            pbest_num = max(1, int(pbest_ratio * 161\t                F = float(np.clip(rng.standard_cauchy() * 0.1 + MF[mi], 0.0, 1.0))\n   162\t                if not np.isfinite(F) or F <= 0.0:\n   163\t                    F = base_F\n   164\t F_vals[i] * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n   199\t\n   200\t                # Binomial crossover\n   201\t                u = np.copy(xi)\n   202\t                j_rand = rng.randint(self.dim)\n   203\t 244\t            # Update SHADE success-history memories\n   245\t            if len(S_F) > 0:\n   246\t                s_f = np.asarray(S_F)\n   247\t                s_cr = np.asarray(S_CR)\n   248\t
